| `auth.agegate.layout.dateEntry` | `{"control":"the Yope form field (groups.create.color.field, size.global.fieldHeight, radius.global.field, groups.create.fontSize.fieldText) with a numeric keyboard","format":"the device locale's numeric short-date order (DateFormatter.dateFormat(fromTemplate: \"MMddyyyy\", options: 0, locale: .current) / DateTimeFormatterBuilder.getLocalizedDateTimePattern(FormatStyle.SHORT, null, IsoChronology.INSTANCE, locale)), shown in the placeholder","default":"empty: no pre-filled date, no default year, no age options","threshold":"the minimum age is never shown before the person submits"}` | FTC COPPA FAQ | birthday entry (neutral age screen) | docs/PLATFORM_CONSTRAINTS.md §9 (FTC COPPA FAQ: a neutral age screen 'allows a user freely to enter month and year of birth'; do not use a 13+-only dropdown or default); Yope form field: references/auto/yope/frames/a-itsmthuyope-7644974799302823175-t49.23-new-group-selected-create.png (https://www.tiktok.com/@itsmthuyope/video/7644974799302823175 @49.23 s, posted 2026-05-28) | VERIFIED | Source is the FTC COPPA FAQ (https://www.ftc.gov/business-guidance/resources/complying-coppa-frequently-asked-questions) via PLATFORM_CONSTRAINTS; Yope's birthday screen was not found (yope-a §2.3). confirm_age needs a full date, so day, month and year are typed. |

## `auth.code`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `auth.code.duration.resendLock` | `60000 ms` | Supabase Auth documentation | time after a code request before 'send new code' is enabled | https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth/phone-login ('By default, a user can only request an OTP once every 60 seconds'), docs/PLATFORM_CONSTRAINTS.md §7 | VERIFIED | No countdown is shown (not observed in Yope); the link is disabled, then enabled. With Twilio Verify, a repeat request inside the code's lifetime returns the same code (PLATFORM_CONSTRAINTS §7). |
| `auth.code.fontSize.textLink` | `14.5 pt` | Yope | lime text link ('send new code'; Yope's sign-in link 'probar otras opciones' = 'try other options') | references/auto/yope/frames/a-virgi-7686278541255642390-t31.23-signup-es.png (https://www.tiktok.com/@virgi.lifestyle/video/7686278541255642390 @31.23 s, posted 2026-09-16) link bbox x262-479 y76-94 | SOURCED | x-height of 'p/r/o' y81-89 = 9 px = 7.6 pt / 0.526 = 14.5 pt; video 576 px wide, creator template: screen x56-519 = 464 px; pt = px / 464 * 393 (1.181 px/pt; 393 pt assumed, device width unknown) · ±2 pt (video). If the recording device was 430/440 pt wide, the true value is 9-12 % larger. · Colour color.global.accent (lime, yope-a §2.1). Weight bold (visual; the frame is dimmed by the system tracking alert, so the colour was not re-measured). |

| `camera.sendto.color.messageField` | `#0000007A` | Yope | translucent fill of the 'add a message' capsule and the mic circle | references/auto/yope/frames/a-chuongxetank-7694968408424582452-t6.73-sendto-voice-tooltip.png (576 x 1024): 'add a message' field over the beige photo: field (90,78,61) at x180-200 y442-448 and (81,68.5,57) at y474-479 vs the photo just above the field (161,144,117) at x180-200 y418-434; field (86,76.5,63) at x200-220 vs photo above (172,158.5,87) | SOURCED | single-background solve; a neutral grey tint g fits the three channels only for g ≤ 0 (g = -47 for the first pair), so the tint is black: alpha = 1 - C/B = 0.44/0.46/0.48 (first pair), 0.50/0.52 (second pair, R/G; its blue channel 0.28 is off because the yellowish photo above differs from the photo under the field) -> mean 0.48 -> #000000 at 0.48 (122/255 = 0x7A) · ±0.05 alpha (video; the blurred photo under the field is assumed equal to the photo just above it; the blur radius is not measured) · Gap fill (MERGE G2-10). Covers the field and the mic circle (same material). Draw it over a backdrop blur of the photo (as observed; radius not measured). Cross-checks in this pass: BeReal's controls over a photo solve to black ≈0.46 (daily.capture.color.overlayControl), Yope's home header circle ≈0.58. |
| `camera.sendto.duration.toastVisible` | `2000 ms` | Locket | how long the confirmation toast stays, and its entrance / exit motion | references/auto/locket/apk/locket-1.243.0-hermes-excerpts.txt (Locket Android 1.243.0, decompiled Hermes JS bundle): Locket's confirmation toasts use ToastAndroid.SHORT; references/auto/platform/aosp-toast-duration-excerpt.txt: SHORT_DELAY = 2000 ms. | `chat.thread.dimension.bubbleMaxWidth` | `256 pt` | Apple Messages (iMessage) | maximum text bubble width before wrapping | references/auto/imessage/apple-support/messages-tapbacks-ios27.png (iOS 27 guide): 3-line incoming bubble body x 84–371 on display x 22–467 (445 px) = 64.7 %; references/auto/google-photos/store/googlephotos-store-13.png (Messages rendered in Google Photos’ App Store panel): 2-line incoming bubble body x 137–832 on display x 88–1154 (1066 px) = 65.2 % | SOURCED | bubble-body width ÷ display width (fill-colour mask, rows 575–630 and 900–1040), × 393 pt. The widest line of a wrapped bubble bounds the maximum from below; the next word that did not fit bounds it from above · lower bound: +0 / +11 % of the screen width (+0 / +43 pt) — tapback image: the next word "good" would have fitted below 76 % · = 0.652 × screen width: scale with the screen (256 pt at 393 pt). Two different texts wrap at 64.7 % and 65.2 % of the display, so the cap sits just above 65 %. Model: iMessage’s group conversation (avatar column beside incoming bubbles, tail-less multi-line bubbles in iOS 27), the closest widely used match for Yope’s group thread (design/MERGE_DECISIONS.md → Gap fill (social) G-1). Yope’s widest single-line bubble (210.7 pt) stays below it. A Yope two-line capture (yope-b §14 capture 1) would supersede this. |

| `chat.thread.layout.failedMessage` | `{"indicator":"outlined exclamation-mark circle beside the bubble on its trailing side, vertically centred on the bubble","indicatorIconIos":"exclamationmark.circle","indicatorIconAndroid":"Rounded.ErrorOutline","indicatorSize":22,"indicatorGap":14,"indicatorColor":"color.global.destructive","indicatorPosition":"the indicator takes the trailing inset slot (chat.thread.dimension.bubbleInsetMine); the bubble moves toward the leading side by indicatorSize + indicatorGap","label":"copy chat.thread.sendFailed under the bubble, trailing-aligned with the message column","labelGap":2,"labelTextRole":"caption","labelColor":"color.global.destructive","retry":"tap the indicator, the label or the bubble: the message is sent again (the label says so); on success the indicator and label go away","haptic":"haptic.global.error when a send fails","appliesTo":"my text and voice messages"}` | Apple Messages (iMessage) | how a message that failed to send is drawn and retried | references/auto/imessage/apple-support/messages-not-delivered.png (support article 118433, iPhone 16 Pro, display x 85–675 = 590 px = 402 pt, 1.468 px/pt): red "!" circle x 617–648 / y 398–430, bubble body x 493–596 / y 384–445, label "Not Delivered" x 511–619 / y 457–468; references/auto/imessage/apple-support/messages-not-delivered-prompt.png (tap → "Try Again"); references/auto/imessage/apple-support/page-118433-not-delivered.png: "Tap the red exclamation point, then tap Try Again." | SOURCED | colour masks (red R>180,G<110,B<110; bubble blue) and bounding boxes; px ÷ 1.468; label size from cap height 12 px ≈ 8 pt ÷ 0.705 ≈ 11 pt · ±1 pt (device mockup at 1.468 px/pt) · Indicator 31–33 px = 22 pt; bubble body → indicator 21 px = 14 pt; bubble → label 3 px = 2 pt; label ≈11 pt, so the 12-pt caption role (text role caption) is used (within ±1 pt). Red: iMessage’s system red = color.global.destructive (same iOS systemRed; role: error state). Messages opens a two-item menu on tap; this product has a single action, which the existing copy ("couldn’t send — tap to try again") already names, so tap retries directly (Gap fill G-4). |

| `chat.voice.layout.recording` | `{"start":"press and hold the mic circle: recording runs while the finger is down","send":"lift the finger: the message is sent","cancel":"slide left while holding: the recording is discarded and nothing is sent","lock":"slide up onto the lock shown above the mic circle: recording continues hands-free","locked":"while locked: chat.voice.icon.pause pauses and lets you play the draft; the mic circle shows icon.global.send and sends","row":"while recording, the composer row shows leading → trailing: red recording dot, elapsed time, the centred hint (‹ slide to cancel); the mic circle stays at the trailing end under the finger","dotColor":"color.global.destructive","timerFormat":"chat.voice.layout.durationFormat","timerColor":"color.global.textPrimary","hintGlyph":"icon.global.back","hintColor":"color.global.textSecondary","hintCopy":"chat.voice.recording.slideToCancel","lockGlyph":"icon.global.lock","lockHintGlyphIos":"chevron.up","lockHintGlyphAndroid":"Rounded.ExpandLess","haptic":"none (HIG: haptics must not disrupt the microphone)"}` | WhatsApp (gesture, documented) + Telegram (row, 2026 panel) | voice recording UI: hold vs tap, lock, cancel, live waveform, timer | references/auto/whatsapp/help/faq-657157755756612-send-voice-iphone.png (verbatim: "Press and hold [mic] next to the text field, then start speaking." / "remove your finger … to automatically send your message. Or, if you’re recording hands-free, tap [pause] to pause recording and preview a draft…" / "slide [‹] to cancel and delete your voice message"); references/auto/whatsapp/store/whatsapp-store-02.png (row: red mic, "0:01", "slide to cancel ‹", mic; lock above); references/auto/telegram/store/telegram-store-03.png (row: red dot, "0:03", "‹ Slide to cancel", enlarged mic circle, lock capsule with chevron up above it) | SOURCED | documented gestures (WhatsApp Help Center, iOS) + element order read from two 2026 App Store panels of two apps · layout rule; sizes not measured (marketing renders) · The two apps agree on hold-to-record, release-to-send, slide-left-to-cancel and a lock above the mic. Red recording dot = color.global.destructive (systemRed, the red both apps use for the recording indicator). Timer and hint text sizes not measured: they take the text role of the field they replace (body). Hint string: copy key chat.voice.recording.slideToCancel (added by the lead after gap fill G-9). |

| `daily.liveactivity.layout.colors` | `{"islandBackground":"system black (not customizable)","lockScreenBackground":"color.global.backgroundDeep","countdown":"color.global.accent","text":"color.global.textPrimary","keyLine":"color.global.accent","systemActionForeground":"color.global.textPrimary"}` | Apple HIG | colors of the Live Activity | https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/live-activities (change log 2026-10-13; screenshot references/auto/platform/hig-live-activities-ios-dimensions.png) §Choosing colors ('Live Activities in the Dynamic Island use a black opaque background'; 'you can use a custom background color for the Lock Screen presentation'; 'Consider using bold colors for text and objects to convey the personality and brand of your app'; 'Tint your Live Activity’s key line color so that it matches your content'); §Lock Screen ('Verify the generated color of the dismiss button') | PLATFORM | quoted rules mapped to the app's foundation colors · The app is dark-only (layout.global.appearance), so the Lock Screen background is the app's black in both system appearances; lime on black passes contrast (design/CONTRAST.md). |

| `haptic.global.error` | `{"ios":"notification.error","android":"REJECT"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | a task failed | HIG Playing haptics (notification feedback); Android HapticFeedbackConstants CONFIRM / REJECT (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations F-26) | PLATFORM | platform mapping · Retro's Android code plays only the tap and tick haptics (no CONFIRM / REJECT, no Vibrator) — design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.8; kept as PLATFORM outcome feedback (MERGE_DECISIONS R-12). |
| `haptic.global.longPressStart` | `{"ios":"system (context menu plays its own haptic; no custom generator)","android":"LONG_PRESS"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | a long press starts (context menu) | HIG Menus / context menus; Android HapticFeedbackConstants.LONG_PRESS (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations) | PLATFORM | platform behaviour |
| `haptic.global.reaction` | `{"ios":"system (context menu plays its own haptic; no custom generator)","android":"LONG_PRESS"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | the long press that opens the reaction picker on a photo | as haptic.global.longPressStart (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations) | PLATFORM | platform behaviour |
| `haptic.global.select` | `{"ios":"selection","android":"SEGMENT_FREQUENT_TICK"}` | Retro | tick: one per step on a dial / scrubber (Rewind dial, friend picker, video trim), a segmented choice, a toggled selection | references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-excerpts.txt 'Tick haptic': SDK ≥ 34 performHapticFeedback(27 SEGMENT_FREQUENT_TICK) else 4 CLOCK_TICK (lh2, e25, f9e, hw, ig, kbh, p6a, pyj); Rewind tab w9e collects the dial flow into the tick helper — design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.8; iOS: HIG Playing haptics 'Selection' (UISelectionFeedbackGenerator) | PLATFORM | call sites in the package; iOS platform equivalent · Haptics.kt maps SEGMENT_FREQUENT_TICK to CLOCK_TICK below API 34, exactly as Retro does. |
| `haptic.global.send` | `{"ios":"notification.success","android":"CONFIRM"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | confirm / send: a photo or message was sent | HIG Playing haptics (notification feedback); Android HapticFeedbackConstants CONFIRM / REJECT (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations F-26) | PLATFORM | platform mapping · Retro's Android code plays only the tap and tick haptics (no CONFIRM / REJECT, no Vibrator) — design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.8; kept as PLATFORM outcome feedback (MERGE_DECISIONS R-12). |
| `haptic.global.shutter` | `{"ios":"none","android":"none"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | shutter press (photo / video capture) | HIG Playing haptics: 'Ensure that haptic vibrations don't disrupt experiences involving … the camera' (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations) | PLATFORM | platform rule |
| `haptic.global.success` | `{"ios":"notification.success","android":"CONFIRM"}` | Apple HIG / Material 3 | a task completed successfully | HIG Playing haptics (notification feedback); Android HapticFeedbackConstants CONFIRM / REJECT (MERGE_DECISIONS Foundations F-26) | PLATFORM | platform mapping · Retro's Android code plays only the tap and tick haptics (no CONFIRM / REJECT, no Vibrator) — design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.8; kept as PLATFORM outcome feedback (MERGE_DECISIONS R-12). |
| `haptic.global.tap` | `{"ios":"none","android":"VIRTUAL_KEY"}` | Retro | tap on a button, tile or card (played just before the action) | references/auto/retro/apk/retro-android-2026.10.1150-excerpts.txt 'Tap haptic wrapper': performHapticFeedback(1 VIRTUAL_KEY) then action (cm9: 17 wrapped click handlers; gd, h21, kbh, w1a, wc1, xk9, n9e) — design/research/retro-package-copy-motion.md §3.8 | PLATFORM | call sites in the package; iOS standard buttons play no haptic (Retro's iOS app was not inspected) |

## `safety.block`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

Locket's confirmation after a safety action (report) is a SHORT platform toast; the same dwell applies to Yope's lime toast everywhere (camera.sendto.duration.toastVisible = 2000). ## `safety.report`

| decision | value | source app | screen / element | evidence | level | method / precision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `safety.report.layout.flow` | `{"entry":"a menu item 'Report…' (ellipsis: more input follows)","ios":"sheet with presentationDetents([.medium, .large]) and a visible grabber: title + explanation (Yope voice), an optional multi-line details field, then 'Report' (destructive role) and 'Cancel' in the navigation bar","android":"ModalBottomSheet (top corners 28 dp, drag handle 32 × 4 dp in onSurfaceVariant at 0.4 opacity, container color.global.system.surfaceContainerLow, initial height ≤ 50 % of the screen) with the same content; 'Report' as a TextButton in color.global.system.error","after":"the sheet content changes to the 'report sent' state; for a person or their content it offers Block (destructive) and Done"}` | Apple HIG | report flow container | https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/action-sheets ; https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/sheets ; https://m3.material.io/components/bottom-sheets/specs ; App Review 1.2 (design/research/platform.md §safety) | VERIFIED | No platform report-reason taxonomy exists (platform §safety 'Not found'); the reason list is safety.report.layout.reasons (BLOCKED). The free-text details field is the shipped path. |
| `safety.report.layout.reasons` | — | Apple HIG | list of report reasons shown before the details field | Not found: neither Apple nor Google publishes a reason taxonomy (design/research/platform.md §safety); Yope's report flow content was not captured (yope-a §2.13). | BLOCKED | Still BLOCKED: the categories are the owner's moderation decision (docs/API.md §12 item 19). Without them, reports carry the optional free text; an empty text sends the target kind as p_reason. Presentation sourced in the gap-fill pass (MERGE G2-8): Locket 1.243.0, a close-friends photo app, reports a friend's photo, message, comment or post with no reason list at all: a native confirmation alert 'Report?' + 'This Locket will be forwarded for review. The owner will not be notified.' with Cancel / Report, then a SHORT toast 'Photo reported' (references/auto/locket/apk/locket-1.243.0-hermes-excerpts.txt). So the flow ships without the list (as today); if the owner adds categories, they are one single-select list of rows above the details field in the existing sheet (safety.report.layout.flow). |

Reduce Motion: unchanged. |

| `easing.year.story.reveal` | `{"x1":0.56,"y1":0.08,"x2":0.14,"y2":0.87}` | Spotify Wrapped | curve of the headline / subline reveal | references/auto/wrapped/frames/wrapped24-rec-emilycraig-reveal-positions.csv (25 headline positions at 30 fps, 15.533-16.333 s, y956 -> 916 px); fit: references/auto/wrapped/frames/wrapped24-rec-emilycraig-reveal-fit.csv | SOURCED | least-squares cubic Bezier over all 25 samples (0.05 grid, then 0.01 grid): rms 0.012 of the travel = 0.48 px, below the 1-px quantization · ±0.05 per control point (flat error surface); residual 0.5 px · Gap fill (MERGE G2-19). This is the headline's curve (duration.year.story.reveal.headline 800 ms, dimension .headlineShift). The subline follows a different, decelerating curve: easing.year.story.reveal.subline (new token). No single curve fits both (best joint fit rms 0.12 = 5-7 px). The earlier 'not fitted' note used only the three summary points (layout.year.story.reveal.samples). Documented curves tried: FastOutSlowIn (0.4,0,0.2,1) rms 0.060, easeInOut 0.112. |
